-- =====================================================================
-- Таблицы проекта «База данных хоккейного клуба Динамо-Минск».
-- Oracle Database 19c. Выполнять от HK_DINAMO.
-- =====================================================================
WHENEVER SQLERROR EXIT SQL.SQLCODE

-- 1. Календарное измерение. TIME_ID имеет формат YYYYMMDD.
CREATE TABLE dim_time (
    time_id       NUMBER(8) CONSTRAINT pk_dim_time PRIMARY KEY,
    full_date     DATE NOT NULL,
    day_of_week   VARCHAR2(20 CHAR) NOT NULL,
    day_num       NUMBER(2) NOT NULL,
    month_num     NUMBER(2) NOT NULL,
    month_name    VARCHAR2(20 CHAR) NOT NULL,
    quarter_num   NUMBER(1) NOT NULL,
    year_num      NUMBER(4) NOT NULL,
    season        VARCHAR2(9 CHAR) NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT uq_dim_time_date UNIQUE (full_date),
    CONSTRAINT ck_dim_time_day CHECK (day_num BETWEEN 1 AND 31),
    CONSTRAINT ck_dim_time_month CHECK (month_num BETWEEN 1 AND 12),
    CONSTRAINT ck_dim_time_quarter CHECK (quarter_num BETWEEN 1 AND 4),
    CONSTRAINT ck_dim_time_year CHECK (year_num BETWEEN 1900 AND 9999),
    CONSTRAINT ck_dim_time_key CHECK (time_id = TO_NUMBER(TO_CHAR(full_date,'YYYYMMDD')))
);
COMMENT ON TABLE dim_time IS 'Календарное измерение для OLAP-анализа по датам и хоккейным сезонам';
COMMENT ON COLUMN dim_time.time_id IS 'Ключ календарной даты в формате YYYYMMDD';
COMMENT ON COLUMN dim_time.season IS 'Хоккейный сезон, например 2026/2027';

-- 2. Справочник стадионов.
CREATE TABLE dim_stadium (
    stadium_id NUMBER GENERATED BY DEFAULT ON NULL AS IDENTITY
               CONSTRAINT pk_dim_stadium PRIMARY KEY,
    name       VARCHAR2(100 CHAR) NOT NULL,
    city       VARCHAR2(50 CHAR) NOT NULL,
    capacity   NUMBER(7) NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT uq_dim_stadium_name_city UNIQUE (name, city),
    CONSTRAINT ck_dim_stadium_capacity CHECK (capacity > 0)
);
COMMENT ON TABLE dim_stadium IS 'Справочник стадионов и арен';

-- 3. Сектора стадионов.
CREATE TABLE dim_sector (
    sector_id   NUMBER GENERATED BY DEFAULT ON NULL AS IDENTITY
                CONSTRAINT pk_dim_sector PRIMARY KEY,
    stadium_id  NUMBER NOT NULL,
    sector_code VARCHAR2(50 CHAR) NOT NULL,
    category    VARCHAR2(20 CHAR) NOT NULL,
    capacity    NUMBER(5) NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT fk_sector_stadium FOREIGN KEY (stadium_id)
        REFERENCES dim_stadium(stadium_id),
    CONSTRAINT uq_sector_stadium_code UNIQUE (stadium_id, sector_code),
    CONSTRAINT ck_dim_sector_capacity CHECK (capacity BETWEEN 1 AND 99999),
    CONSTRAINT ck_dim_sector_category CHECK
        (category IN ('VIP','STANDARD','FAN','GUEST','OTHER'))
);
COMMENT ON TABLE dim_sector IS 'Сектора стадионов с вместимостью и категорией';

-- 4. Справочник игроков.
CREATE TABLE dim_player (
    player_id     NUMBER GENERATED BY DEFAULT ON NULL AS IDENTITY
                  CONSTRAINT pk_dim_player PRIMARY KEY,
    first_name    VARCHAR2(50 CHAR) NOT NULL,
    last_name     VARCHAR2(50 CHAR) NOT NULL,
    position      VARCHAR2(15 CHAR) NOT NULL,
    jersey_number NUMBER(2),
    birth_date    DATE,
    country       VARCHAR2(50 CHAR),
    height_cm     NUMBER(3),
    weight_kg     NUMBER(3),
    is_active     CHAR(1) DEFAULT 'Y' NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT ck_dim_player_position CHECK
        (position IN ('GOALTENDER','DEFENDER','FORWARD')),
    CONSTRAINT ck_dim_player_jersey CHECK
        (jersey_number IS NULL OR jersey_number BETWEEN 0 AND 99),
    CONSTRAINT ck_dim_player_height CHECK
        (height_cm IS NULL OR height_cm BETWEEN 100 AND 250),
    CONSTRAINT ck_dim_player_weight CHECK
        (weight_kg IS NULL OR weight_kg BETWEEN 30 AND 200),
    CONSTRAINT ck_dim_player_active CHECK (is_active IN ('Y','N'))
);
COMMENT ON TABLE dim_player IS 'Справочник игроков хоккейного клуба';
COMMENT ON COLUMN dim_player.position IS 'GOALTENDER — вратарь; DEFENDER — защитник; FORWARD — нападающий';
COMMENT ON COLUMN dim_player.is_active IS 'Y — активный игрок; N — неактивный игрок';

-- 5. Матчи клуба.
CREATE TABLE dim_match (
    match_id      NUMBER GENERATED BY DEFAULT ON NULL AS IDENTITY
                  CONSTRAINT pk_dim_match PRIMARY KEY,
    match_date    DATE NOT NULL,
    time_id       NUMBER(8) NOT NULL,
    opponent_name VARCHAR2(100 CHAR) NOT NULL,
    stadium_id    NUMBER NOT NULL,
    is_home       CHAR(1) NOT NULL,
    goals_for     NUMBER(2),
    goals_against NUMBER(2),
    match_status  VARCHAR2(15 CHAR) DEFAULT 'SCHEDULED' NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT fk_match_time FOREIGN KEY (time_id) REFERENCES dim_time(time_id),
    CONSTRAINT fk_match_stadium FOREIGN KEY (stadium_id) REFERENCES dim_stadium(stadium_id),
    CONSTRAINT ck_dim_match_home CHECK (is_home IN ('Y','N')),
    CONSTRAINT ck_dim_match_goals CHECK
       ((goals_for IS NULL AND goals_against IS NULL) OR
        (goals_for IS NOT NULL AND goals_against IS NOT NULL AND
         goals_for >= 0 AND goals_against >= 0)),
    CONSTRAINT ck_dim_match_status CHECK
       (match_status IN ('SCHEDULED','COMPLETED','CANCELLED')),
    CONSTRAINT ck_dim_match_completed CHECK
       (match_status <> 'COMPLETED' OR
        (goals_for IS NOT NULL AND goals_against IS NOT NULL))
);
COMMENT ON TABLE dim_match IS 'Матчи: дата, соперник, арена, статус и итоговый счёт';

-- 6. Болельщики и покупатели.
CREATE TABLE dim_fan (
    fan_id            NUMBER GENERATED BY DEFAULT ON NULL AS IDENTITY
                      CONSTRAINT pk_dim_fan PRIMARY KEY,
    first_name        VARCHAR2(50 CHAR) NOT NULL,
    last_name         VARCHAR2(50 CHAR) NOT NULL,
    email             VARCHAR2(100 CHAR),
    phone             VARCHAR2(20 CHAR),
    city              VARCHAR2(50 CHAR),
    registration_date DATE DEFAULT SYSDATE NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT uq_dim_fan_email UNIQUE (email)
);
COMMENT ON TABLE dim_fan IS 'Справочник болельщиков и покупателей';

-- 7. Каталог клубной атрибутики.
CREATE TABLE dim_merch (
    merch_id    NUMBER GENERATED BY DEFAULT ON NULL AS IDENTITY
                CONSTRAINT pk_dim_merch PRIMARY KEY,
    name        VARCHAR2(100 CHAR) NOT NULL,
    category    VARCHAR2(50 CHAR) NOT NULL,
    price       NUMBER(10,2) NOT NULL,
    description VARCHAR2(500 CHAR),
    is_active   CHAR(1) DEFAULT 'Y' NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT ck_dim_merch_price CHECK (price >= 0),
    CONSTRAINT ck_dim_merch_active CHECK (is_active IN ('Y','N'))
);
COMMENT ON TABLE dim_merch IS 'Каталог товаров клубной атрибутики';

-- 8. Билеты. Одна строка — одно место на конкретный матч.
CREATE TABLE tickets (
    ticket_id      NUMBER GENERATED BY DEFAULT ON NULL AS IDENTITY
                   CONSTRAINT pk_tickets PRIMARY KEY,
    match_id       NUMBER NOT NULL,
    sector_id      NUMBER NOT NULL,
    seat_row       NUMBER(3) NOT NULL,
    seat_number    NUMBER(3) NOT NULL,
    price          NUMBER(10,2) NOT NULL,
    status         VARCHAR2(10 CHAR) DEFAULT 'FREE' NOT NULL,
    fan_id         NUMBER,
    sale_time_id   NUMBER(8),
    reserved_by    NUMBER,
    reserved_until DATE,
    created_at     TIMESTAMP DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP NOT NULL,
    updated_at     TIMESTAMP DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT fk_ticket_match FOREIGN KEY (match_id) REFERENCES dim_match(match_id),
    CONSTRAINT fk_ticket_sector FOREIGN KEY (sector_id) REFERENCES dim_sector(sector_id),
    CONSTRAINT fk_ticket_fan FOREIGN KEY (fan_id) REFERENCES dim_fan(fan_id),
    CONSTRAINT fk_ticket_sale_time FOREIGN KEY (sale_time_id) REFERENCES dim_time(time_id),
    CONSTRAINT fk_ticket_reserved_by FOREIGN KEY (reserved_by) REFERENCES dim_fan(fan_id),
    CONSTRAINT uq_ticket_seat UNIQUE (match_id, sector_id, seat_row, seat_number),
    CONSTRAINT ck_ticket_seat CHECK (seat_row > 0 AND seat_number > 0),
    CONSTRAINT ck_ticket_price CHECK (price >= 0),
    CONSTRAINT ck_ticket_status CHECK (status IN ('FREE','RESERVED','SOLD','RETURNED')),
    CONSTRAINT ck_ticket_state_fields CHECK (
       (status = 'FREE' AND fan_id IS NULL AND sale_time_id IS NULL
          AND reserved_by IS NULL AND reserved_until IS NULL)
       OR
       (status = 'RESERVED' AND fan_id IS NULL AND sale_time_id IS NULL
          AND reserved_by IS NOT NULL AND reserved_until IS NOT NULL)
       OR
       (status = 'SOLD' AND fan_id IS NOT NULL AND sale_time_id IS NOT NULL
          AND reserved_by IS NULL AND reserved_until IS NULL)
       OR
       (status = 'RETURNED' AND reserved_by IS NULL AND reserved_until IS NULL)
    )
);
COMMENT ON TABLE tickets IS 'Билеты: учёт мест, продаж, возвратов и временного резервирования';
COMMENT ON COLUMN tickets.status IS 'FREE — свободен; RESERVED — зарезервирован; SOLD — продан; RETURNED — возвращён';
COMMENT ON COLUMN tickets.sale_time_id IS 'Дата продажи для анализа выручки по периодам';
COMMENT ON COLUMN tickets.reserved_until IS 'Дата и время окончания резерва';

-- 9. Продажи атрибутики. Одна строка — одна товарная позиция.
CREATE TABLE merch_orders (
    order_id     NUMBER GENERATED BY DEFAULT ON NULL AS IDENTITY
                 CONSTRAINT pk_merch_orders PRIMARY KEY,
    merch_id     NUMBER NOT NULL,
    fan_id       NUMBER NOT NULL,
    time_id      NUMBER(8) NOT NULL,
    quantity     NUMBER(5) NOT NULL,
    unit_price   NUMBER(10,2) NOT NULL,
    total_amount NUMBER(12,2) GENERATED ALWAYS AS (quantity * unit_price) VIRTUAL,
    status       VARCHAR2(20 CHAR) DEFAULT 'COMPLETED' NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT fk_merch_order_merch FOREIGN KEY (merch_id) REFERENCES dim_merch(merch_id),
    CONSTRAINT fk_merch_order_fan FOREIGN KEY (fan_id) REFERENCES dim_fan(fan_id),
    CONSTRAINT fk_merch_order_time FOREIGN KEY (time_id) REFERENCES dim_time(time_id),
    CONSTRAINT ck_merch_order_qty CHECK (quantity > 0),
    CONSTRAINT ck_merch_order_price CHECK (unit_price >= 0),
    CONSTRAINT ck_merch_order_status CHECK (status IN ('PENDING','COMPLETED','CANCELLED'))
);
COMMENT ON TABLE merch_orders IS 'Факты продаж атрибутики; одна строка — один вид товара в заказе';
COMMENT ON COLUMN merch_orders.total_amount IS 'Виртуальный столбец: quantity * unit_price';

-- 10. Статистика игроков по матчам.
CREATE TABLE fact_match_stats (
    stat_id         NUMBER GENERATED BY DEFAULT ON NULL AS IDENTITY
                    CONSTRAINT pk_fact_match_stats PRIMARY KEY,
    match_id        NUMBER NOT NULL,
    player_id       NUMBER NOT NULL,
    time_id         NUMBER(8) NOT NULL,
    goals           NUMBER(2) DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL,
    assists         NUMBER(2) DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL,
    points          NUMBER(3) GENERATED ALWAYS AS (goals + assists) VIRTUAL,
    penalty_minutes NUMBER(3) DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL,
    plus_minus      NUMBER(3) DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL,
    shots           NUMBER(3) DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL,
    time_on_ice_sec NUMBER(5) DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT fk_stats_match FOREIGN KEY (match_id) REFERENCES dim_match(match_id),
    CONSTRAINT fk_stats_player FOREIGN KEY (player_id) REFERENCES dim_player(player_id),
    CONSTRAINT fk_stats_time FOREIGN KEY (time_id) REFERENCES dim_time(time_id),
    CONSTRAINT uq_match_player UNIQUE (match_id, player_id),
    CONSTRAINT ck_stats_goals CHECK (goals >= 0),
    CONSTRAINT ck_stats_assists CHECK (assists >= 0),
    CONSTRAINT ck_stats_penalty CHECK (penalty_minutes >= 0),
    CONSTRAINT ck_stats_shots CHECK (shots >= 0),
    CONSTRAINT ck_stats_ice_time CHECK (time_on_ice_sec >= 0)
);
COMMENT ON TABLE fact_match_stats IS 'Статистика игроков по матчам для анализа результативности';
COMMENT ON COLUMN fact_match_stats.points IS 'Виртуальный столбец: голы плюс передачи';

-- 11. Учётные записи приложения. FAN_ID связывает профиль пользователя
-- приложения с записью болельщика для операций с билетами и избранным.
CREATE TABLE app_users (
    user_id       NUMBER GENERATED BY DEFAULT ON NULL AS IDENTITY
                  CONSTRAINT pk_app_users PRIMARY KEY,
    username      VARCHAR2(50 CHAR) NOT NULL,
    password_hash VARCHAR2(200 CHAR) NOT NULL,
    email         VARCHAR2(100 CHAR) NOT NULL,
    role          VARCHAR2(20 CHAR) NOT NULL,
    fan_id        NUMBER,
    db_username   VARCHAR2(128 CHAR),
    is_active     CHAR(1) DEFAULT 'Y' NOT NULL,
    created_at    DATE DEFAULT SYSDATE NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT fk_app_users_fan FOREIGN KEY (fan_id) REFERENCES dim_fan(fan_id),
    CONSTRAINT uq_app_users_username UNIQUE (username),
    CONSTRAINT uq_app_users_email UNIQUE (email),
    CONSTRAINT uq_app_users_fan UNIQUE (fan_id),
    CONSTRAINT uq_app_users_db_user UNIQUE (db_username),
    CONSTRAINT ck_app_users_role CHECK (role IN ('MAIN_ADMIN','ADMIN','USER')),
    CONSTRAINT ck_app_users_active CHECK (is_active IN ('Y','N'))
);
COMMENT ON TABLE app_users IS 'Прикладные учётные записи; не заменяют учётные записи Oracle';
COMMENT ON COLUMN app_users.password_hash IS 'Хеш пароля, сформированный прикладной системой; открытый пароль не хранится';
COMMENT ON COLUMN app_users.fan_id IS 'Необязательная связь прикладной учётной записи с профилем болельщика';
COMMENT ON COLUMN app_users.db_username IS 'Необязательная привязка к Oracle-пользователю для проверки демонстрационного подключения';

-- 12. Связь болельщиков с избранными игроками для оценки популярности.
CREATE TABLE fan_player_favorites (
    favorite_id NUMBER GENERATED BY DEFAULT ON NULL AS IDENTITY
                CONSTRAINT pk_fan_player_favorites PRIMARY KEY,
    fan_id      NUMBER NOT NULL,
    player_id   NUMBER NOT NULL,
    created_at  TIMESTAMP DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT fk_favorites_fan FOREIGN KEY (fan_id) REFERENCES dim_fan(fan_id),
    CONSTRAINT fk_favorites_player FOREIGN KEY (player_id) REFERENCES dim_player(player_id),
    CONSTRAINT uq_fan_player_favorite UNIQUE (fan_id, player_id)
);
COMMENT ON TABLE fan_player_favorites IS 'Избранные игроки; используется для оценки популярности';

PROMPT Таблицы и ограничения созданы.


================================================================================
FILE: HK_DINAMO_Oracle19c\02_logic\02_triggers.sql
================================================================================
-- =====================================================================
-- Триггеры контроля согласованности и автоматического заполнения дат.
-- =====================================================================
WHENEVER SQLERROR EXIT SQL.SQLCODE

CREATE OR REPLACE TRIGGER trg_dim_match_biu
BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE OF match_date, time_id ON dim_match
FOR EACH ROW
DECLARE
    l_full_date DATE;
BEGIN
    SELECT full_date INTO l_full_date
      FROM dim_time
     WHERE time_id = :NEW.time_id;
    IF TRUNC(l_full_date) <> TRUNC(:NEW.match_date) THEN
        RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20001,
            'Дата матча должна совпадать с датой календарного измерения DIM_TIME');
    END IF;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE TRIGGER trg_tickets_biu
BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON tickets
FOR EACH ROW
DECLARE
    l_match_stadium  NUMBER;
    l_sector_stadium NUMBER;
BEGIN
    IF INSERTING THEN
        :NEW.created_at := COALESCE(:NEW.created_at, SYSTIMESTAMP);
        :NEW.updated_at := SYSTIMESTAMP;
    ELSE
        :NEW.created_at := :OLD.created_at;
        :NEW.updated_at := SYSTIMESTAMP;

        IF UPDATING('STATUS') AND :NEW.status <> :OLD.status THEN
            IF NOT (
                (:OLD.status = 'FREE'     AND :NEW.status IN ('RESERVED','SOLD')) OR
                (:OLD.status = 'RESERVED' AND :NEW.status IN ('FREE','SOLD')) OR
                (:OLD.status = 'SOLD'     AND :NEW.status = 'RETURNED') OR
                (:OLD.status = 'RETURNED' AND :NEW.status IN ('RESERVED','SOLD'))
            ) THEN
                RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20002,
                    'Недопустимый переход статуса билета');
            END IF;
        END IF;
    END IF;

    -- Сектор должен принадлежать тому же стадиону, на котором проводится матч.
    SELECT m.stadium_id, s.stadium_id
      INTO l_match_stadium, l_sector_stadium
      FROM dim_match m CROSS JOIN dim_sector s
     WHERE m.match_id = :NEW.match_id
       AND s.sector_id = :NEW.sector_id;

    IF l_match_stadium <> l_sector_stadium THEN
        RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20003,
            'Выбранный сектор не относится к стадиону указанного матча');
    END IF;
END;
/

PROMPT Триггеры TRG_DIM_MATCH_BIU и TRG_TICKETS_BIU созданы.


================================================================================
FILE: HK_DINAMO_Oracle19c\02_logic\03_functions.sql
================================================================================
-- =====================================================================
-- Функции для аналитических показателей.
-- =====================================================================
WHENEVER SQLERROR EXIT SQL.SQLCODE

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION f_ticket_revenue(
    p_date_from IN DATE,
    p_date_to   IN DATE
) RETURN NUMBER
AUTHID DEFINER
IS
    l_revenue NUMBER(14,2);
BEGIN
    IF p_date_from IS NULL OR p_date_to IS NULL OR TRUNC(p_date_from) > TRUNC(p_date_to) THEN
        RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20010, 'Некорректный диапазон дат для расчёта выручки');
    END IF;

    SELECT NVL(SUM(t.price), 0)
      INTO l_revenue
      FROM tickets t
      JOIN dim_time d ON d.time_id = t.sale_time_id
     WHERE t.status = 'SOLD'
       AND d.full_date >= TRUNC(p_date_from)
       AND d.full_date < TRUNC(p_date_to) + 1;

    RETURN l_revenue;
END;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION f_player_popularity(
    p_player_id IN NUMBER
) RETURN NUMBER
AUTHID DEFINER
IS
    l_count NUMBER;
BEGIN
    SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT fan_id)
      INTO l_count
      FROM fan_player_favorites
     WHERE player_id = p_player_id;
    RETURN l_count;
END;
/

PROMPT Функции F_TICKET_REVENUE и F_PLAYER_POPULARITY созданы.


================================================================================
FILE: HK_DINAMO_Oracle19c\02_logic\04_views.sql
================================================================================
-- =====================================================================
-- Представления для аналитических запросов и отчётности.
-- =====================================================================
WHENEVER SQLERROR EXIT SQL.SQLCODE

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW v_ticket_sales_by_period AS
SELECT d.full_date,
       d.year_num,
       d.month_num,
       d.month_name,
       d.season,
       COUNT(*) AS tickets_sold,
       SUM(t.price) AS revenue
  FROM tickets t
  JOIN dim_time d ON d.time_id = t.sale_time_id
 WHERE t.status = 'SOLD'
 GROUP BY d.full_date, d.year_num, d.month_num, d.month_name, d.season;

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW v_player_popularity AS
SELECT p.player_id,
       p.first_name,
       p.last_name,
       COUNT(DISTINCT f.fan_id) AS fans_favorited
  FROM dim_player p
  LEFT JOIN fan_player_favorites f ON f.player_id = p.player_id
 GROUP BY p.player_id, p.first_name, p.last_name;

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW v_player_performance AS
SELECT p.player_id,
       p.first_name,
       p.last_name,
       COUNT(s.stat_id) AS matches_count,
       NVL(SUM(s.goals), 0) AS goals_total,
       NVL(SUM(s.assists), 0) AS assists_total,
       NVL(SUM(s.points), 0) AS points_total,
       NVL(SUM(s.shots), 0) AS shots_total,
       NVL(SUM(s.penalty_minutes), 0) AS penalty_minutes_total
  FROM dim_player p
  LEFT JOIN fact_match_stats s ON s.player_id = p.player_id
 GROUP BY p.player_id, p.first_name, p.last_name;

CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW v_merch_sales_by_period AS
SELECT d.full_date,
       d.year_num,
       d.month_num,
       d.season,
       m.merch_id,
       m.name AS merch_name,
       SUM(o.quantity) AS quantity_sold,
       SUM(o.total_amount) AS revenue
  FROM merch_orders o
  JOIN dim_time d ON d.time_id = o.time_id
  JOIN dim_merch m ON m.merch_id = o.merch_id
 WHERE o.status = 'COMPLETED'
 GROUP BY d.full_date, d.year_num, d.month_num, d.season, m.merch_id, m.name;

PROMPT Представления V_TICKET_SALES_BY_PERIOD, V_PLAYER_POPULARITY,
PROMPT V_PLAYER_PERFORMANCE и V_MERCH_SALES_BY_PERIOD созданы.


================================================================================
FILE: HK_DINAMO_Oracle19c\02_logic\05_packages.sql
================================================================================
-- =====================================================================
-- Пакеты серверной логики: пользовательские операции, администрирование,
-- управление прикладными учётными записями и JSON-импорт/экспорт.
-- Все пакеты работают с правами владельца (AUTHID DEFINER).
-- =====================================================================
WHENEVER SQLERROR EXIT SQL.SQLCODE

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Пакет административных операций с предметными данными.
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE OR REPLACE PACKAGE pkg_admin_api AUTHID DEFINER AS
    PROCEDURE add_player(
        p_first_name IN VARCHAR2, p_last_name IN VARCHAR2,
        p_position IN VARCHAR2, p_jersey_number IN NUMBER,
        p_birth_date IN DATE, p_country IN VARCHAR2,
        p_height_cm IN NUMBER, p_weight_kg IN NUMBER,
        p_player_id OUT NUMBER);

    PROCEDURE update_player(
        p_player_id IN NUMBER, p_first_name IN VARCHAR2,
        p_last_name IN VARCHAR2, p_position IN VARCHAR2,
        p_jersey_number IN NUMBER, p_birth_date IN DATE,
        p_country IN VARCHAR2, p_height_cm IN NUMBER,
        p_weight_kg IN NUMBER, p_is_active IN CHAR);

    PROCEDURE delete_player(p_player_id IN NUMBER);

    PROCEDURE add_match(
        p_match_date IN DATE, p_opponent_name IN VARCHAR2,
        p_stadium_id IN NUMBER, p_is_home IN CHAR,
        p_match_id OUT NUMBER);

    PROCEDURE update_match(
        p_match_id IN NUMBER, p_match_date IN DATE,
        p_opponent_name IN VARCHAR2, p_stadium_id IN NUMBER,
        p_is_home IN CHAR, p_match_status IN VARCHAR2,
        p_goals_for IN NUMBER, p_goals_against IN NUMBER);

    PROCEDURE delete_match(p_match_id IN NUMBER);

    PROCEDURE add_ticket(
        p_match_id IN NUMBER, p_sector_id IN NUMBER,
        p_seat_row IN NUMBER, p_seat_number IN NUMBER,
        p_price IN NUMBER, p_ticket_id OUT NUMBER);

    PROCEDURE sell_ticket(p_ticket_id IN NUMBER, p_fan_id IN NUMBER);
    PROCEDURE return_ticket(p_ticket_id IN NUMBER);
    PROCEDURE release_expired_reservations(p_released_count OUT NUMBER);

    PROCEDURE add_merch(
        p_name IN VARCHAR2, p_category IN VARCHAR2,
        p_price IN NUMBER, p_description IN VARCHAR2,
        p_merch_id OUT NUMBER);

    PROCEDURE update_merch(
        p_merch_id IN NUMBER, p_name IN VARCHAR2,
        p_category IN VARCHAR2, p_price IN NUMBER,
        p_description IN VARCHAR2, p_is_active IN CHAR);

    PROCEDURE delete_merch(p_merch_id IN NUMBER);

    PROCEDURE add_merch_order(
        p_merch_id IN NUMBER, p_fan_id IN NUMBER,
        p_time_id IN NUMBER, p_quantity IN NUMBER,
        p_order_id OUT NUMBER);

    PROCEDURE ticket_sales_report(p_date_from IN DATE, p_date_to IN DATE,
                                  p_result OUT SYS_REFCURSOR);
    PROCEDURE player_popularity_report(p_result OUT SYS_REFCURSOR);
END pkg_admin_api;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE PACKAGE BODY pkg_admin_api AS
    PROCEDURE add_player(
        p_first_name IN VARCHAR2, p_last_name IN VARCHAR2,
        p_position IN VARCHAR2, p_jersey_number IN NUMBER,
        p_birth_date IN DATE, p_country IN VARCHAR2,
        p_height_cm IN NUMBER, p_weight_kg IN NUMBER,
        p_player_id OUT NUMBER) IS
    BEGIN
        INSERT INTO dim_player(first_name,last_name,position,jersey_number,
                               birth_date,country,height_cm,weight_kg)
        VALUES (TRIM(p_first_name),TRIM(p_last_name),UPPER(TRIM(p_position)),
                p_jersey_number,p_birth_date,p_country,p_height_cm,p_weight_kg)
        RETURNING player_id INTO p_player_id;
    END;

    PROCEDURE update_player(
        p_player_id IN NUMBER, p_first_name IN VARCHAR2,
        p_last_name IN VARCHAR2, p_position IN VARCHAR2,
        p_jersey_number IN NUMBER, p_birth_date IN DATE,
        p_country IN VARCHAR2, p_height_cm IN NUMBER,
        p_weight_kg IN NUMBER, p_is_active IN CHAR) IS
    BEGIN
        UPDATE dim_player
           SET first_name=TRIM(p_first_name), last_name=TRIM(p_last_name),
               position=UPPER(TRIM(p_position)), jersey_number=p_jersey_number,
               birth_date=p_birth_date, country=p_country,
               height_cm=p_height_cm, weight_kg=p_weight_kg,
               is_active=UPPER(p_is_active)
         WHERE player_id=p_player_id;
        IF SQL%ROWCOUNT=0 THEN
            RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20020,'Игрок не найден');
        END IF;
    END;

    PROCEDURE delete_player(p_player_id IN NUMBER) IS
    BEGIN
        DELETE FROM dim_player WHERE player_id=p_player_id;
        IF SQL%ROWCOUNT=0 THEN
            RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20021,'Игрок не найден');
        END IF;
        -- Если игрок уже присутствует в статистике или избранном,
        -- Oracle запретит удаление по внешнему ключу. В таком случае
        -- рекомендуется использовать UPDATE is_active='N'.
    END;

    PROCEDURE add_match(
        p_match_date IN DATE, p_opponent_name IN VARCHAR2,
        p_stadium_id IN NUMBER, p_is_home IN CHAR,
        p_match_id OUT NUMBER) IS
        l_time_id NUMBER(8);
    BEGIN
        SELECT time_id INTO l_time_id
          FROM dim_time
         WHERE full_date=TRUNC(p_match_date);
        INSERT INTO dim_match(match_date,time_id,opponent_name,stadium_id,is_home)
        VALUES(p_match_date,l_time_id,TRIM(p_opponent_name),p_stadium_id,UPPER(p_is_home))
        RETURNING match_id INTO p_match_id;
    EXCEPTION
        WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN
            RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20022,'Для даты матча отсутствует строка в DIM_TIME');
    END;

    PROCEDURE update_match(
        p_match_id IN NUMBER, p_match_date IN DATE,
        p_opponent_name IN VARCHAR2, p_stadium_id IN NUMBER,
        p_is_home IN CHAR, p_match_status IN VARCHAR2,
        p_goals_for IN NUMBER, p_goals_against IN NUMBER) IS
        l_time_id NUMBER(8);
    BEGIN
        SELECT time_id INTO l_time_id
          FROM dim_time
         WHERE full_date=TRUNC(p_match_date);
        UPDATE dim_match
           SET match_date=p_match_date, time_id=l_time_id,
               opponent_name=TRIM(p_opponent_name), stadium_id=p_stadium_id,
               is_home=UPPER(p_is_home), match_status=UPPER(p_match_status),
               goals_for=p_goals_for, goals_against=p_goals_against
         WHERE match_id=p_match_id;
        IF SQL%ROWCOUNT=0 THEN
            RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20023,'Матч не найден');
        END IF;
    EXCEPTION
        WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN
            RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20022,'Для даты матча отсутствует строка в DIM_TIME');
    END;

    PROCEDURE delete_match(p_match_id IN NUMBER) IS
    BEGIN
        DELETE FROM dim_match WHERE match_id=p_match_id;
        IF SQL%ROWCOUNT=0 THEN
            RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20024,'Матч не найден');
        END IF;
    END;

    PROCEDURE add_ticket(
        p_match_id IN NUMBER, p_sector_id IN NUMBER,
        p_seat_row IN NUMBER, p_seat_number IN NUMBER,
        p_price IN NUMBER, p_ticket_id OUT NUMBER) IS
    BEGIN
        INSERT INTO tickets(match_id,sector_id,seat_row,seat_number,price,status)
        VALUES(p_match_id,p_sector_id,p_seat_row,p_seat_number,p_price,'FREE')
        RETURNING ticket_id INTO p_ticket_id;
    END;

    PROCEDURE sell_ticket(p_ticket_id IN NUMBER, p_fan_id IN NUMBER) IS
        l_status VARCHAR2(10);
        l_time_id NUMBER(8);
    BEGIN
        SELECT status INTO l_status
          FROM tickets
         WHERE ticket_id=p_ticket_id
           FOR UPDATE;
        IF l_status NOT IN ('FREE','RESERVED','RETURNED') THEN
            RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20025,'Билет нельзя продать в текущем статусе');
        END IF;
        SELECT time_id INTO l_time_id
          FROM dim_time
         WHERE full_date=TRUNC(SYSDATE);
        UPDATE tickets
           SET status='SOLD', fan_id=p_fan_id, sale_time_id=l_time_id,
               reserved_by=NULL, reserved_until=NULL
         WHERE ticket_id=p_ticket_id;
    EXCEPTION
        WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN
            RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20026,'Билет не найден или текущая дата отсутствует в DIM_TIME');
    END;

    PROCEDURE return_ticket(p_ticket_id IN NUMBER) IS
        l_status VARCHAR2(10);
    BEGIN
        SELECT status INTO l_status
          FROM tickets
         WHERE ticket_id=p_ticket_id
           FOR UPDATE;
        IF l_status <> 'SOLD' THEN
            RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20027,'Возврат возможен только для проданного билета');
        END IF;
        UPDATE tickets
           SET status='RETURNED', reserved_by=NULL, reserved_until=NULL
         WHERE ticket_id=p_ticket_id;
        -- fan_id и sale_time_id сохраняются как сведения о состоявшейся продаже.
    EXCEPTION
        WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN
            RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20026,'Билет не найден');
    END;

    PROCEDURE release_expired_reservations(p_released_count OUT NUMBER) IS
    BEGIN
        UPDATE tickets
           SET status='FREE', reserved_by=NULL, reserved_until=NULL
         WHERE status='RESERVED' AND reserved_until <= SYSDATE;
        p_released_count := SQL%ROWCOUNT;
    END;

    PROCEDURE add_merch(
        p_name IN VARCHAR2, p_category IN VARCHAR2,
        p_price IN NUMBER, p_description IN VARCHAR2,
        p_merch_id OUT NUMBER) IS
    BEGIN
        INSERT INTO dim_merch(name,category,price,description)
        VALUES(TRIM(p_name),TRIM(p_category),p_price,p_description)
        RETURNING merch_id INTO p_merch_id;
    END;

    PROCEDURE update_merch(
        p_merch_id IN NUMBER, p_name IN VARCHAR2,
        p_category IN VARCHAR2, p_price IN NUMBER,
        p_description IN VARCHAR2, p_is_active IN CHAR) IS
    BEGIN
        UPDATE dim_merch
           SET name=TRIM(p_name), category=TRIM(p_category), price=p_price,
               description=p_description, is_active=UPPER(p_is_active)
         WHERE merch_id=p_merch_id;
        IF SQL%ROWCOUNT=0 THEN
            RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20028,'Товар не найден');
        END IF;
    END;

    PROCEDURE delete_merch(p_merch_id IN NUMBER) IS
    BEGIN
        DELETE FROM dim_merch WHERE merch_id=p_merch_id;
        IF SQL%ROWCOUNT=0 THEN
            RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20029,'Товар не найден');
        END IF;
    END;

    PROCEDURE add_merch_order(
        p_merch_id IN NUMBER, p_fan_id IN NUMBER,
        p_time_id IN NUMBER, p_quantity IN NUMBER,
        p_order_id OUT NUMBER) IS
        l_price NUMBER(10,2);
    BEGIN
        SELECT price INTO l_price
          FROM dim_merch
         WHERE merch_id=p_merch_id AND is_active='Y';
        INSERT INTO merch_orders(merch_id,fan_id,time_id,quantity,unit_price,status)
        VALUES(p_merch_id,p_fan_id,p_time_id,p_quantity,l_price,'COMPLETED')
        RETURNING order_id INTO p_order_id;
    EXCEPTION
        WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN
            RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20030,'Активный товар не найден');
    END;

    PROCEDURE ticket_sales_report(p_date_from IN DATE, p_date_to IN DATE,
                                  p_result OUT SYS_REFCURSOR) IS
    BEGIN
        OPEN p_result FOR
            SELECT full_date, year_num, month_num, month_name, season,
                   tickets_sold, revenue
              FROM v_ticket_sales_by_period
             WHERE (p_date_from IS NULL OR full_date >= TRUNC(p_date_from))
               AND (p_date_to IS NULL OR full_date < TRUNC(p_date_to)+1)
             ORDER BY full_date;
    END;

    PROCEDURE player_popularity_report(p_result OUT SYS_REFCURSOR) IS
    BEGIN
        OPEN p_result FOR
            SELECT player_id, first_name, last_name, fans_favorited
              FROM v_player_popularity
             ORDER BY fans_favorited DESC, last_name, first_name;
    END;
END pkg_admin_api;
/

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Пакет действий пользователя. Профиль болельщика определяется по
-- APP_USERS.FAN_ID после проверки активной прикладной записи с ролью USER.
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE OR REPLACE PACKAGE pkg_user_api AUTHID DEFINER AS
    PROCEDURE list_players(p_result OUT SYS_REFCURSOR);
    PROCEDURE list_matches(p_date_from IN DATE, p_date_to IN DATE,
                           p_result OUT SYS_REFCURSOR);
    PROCEDURE list_available_tickets(p_match_id IN NUMBER,
                                     p_result OUT SYS_REFCURSOR);
    PROCEDURE list_merch(p_result OUT SYS_REFCURSOR);
    PROCEDURE reserve_ticket(p_ticket_id IN NUMBER, p_app_user_id IN NUMBER,
                             p_minutes IN NUMBER DEFAULT 15);
    PROCEDURE add_favorite(p_app_user_id IN NUMBER, p_player_id IN NUMBER);
    PROCEDURE remove_favorite(p_app_user_id IN NUMBER, p_player_id IN NUMBER);
    PROCEDURE list_favorites(p_app_user_id IN NUMBER, p_result OUT SYS_REFCURSOR);
END pkg_user_api;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE PACKAGE BODY pkg_user_api AS
    FUNCTION current_fan_id(p_app_user_id IN NUMBER) RETURN NUMBER IS
        l_fan_id NUMBER;
    BEGIN
        SELECT fan_id INTO l_fan_id
          FROM app_users
         WHERE user_id=p_app_user_id
           AND is_active='Y'
           AND role='USER'
           AND fan_id IS NOT NULL
           AND UPPER(db_username)=SYS_CONTEXT('USERENV','SESSION_USER');
        RETURN l_fan_id;
    EXCEPTION
        WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN
            RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20040,
              'Для текущего Oracle-сеанса не найдена активная APP_USERS-запись USER с привязанным DIM_FAN');
    END;

    PROCEDURE list_players(p_result OUT SYS_REFCURSOR) IS
    BEGIN
        OPEN p_result FOR
            SELECT player_id, first_name, last_name, position, jersey_number,
                   birth_date, country, is_active
              FROM dim_player
             WHERE is_active='Y'
             ORDER BY last_name, first_name;
    END;

    PROCEDURE list_matches(p_date_from IN DATE, p_date_to IN DATE,
                           p_result OUT SYS_REFCURSOR) IS
    BEGIN
        OPEN p_result FOR
            SELECT m.match_id, m.match_date, m.opponent_name, s.name AS stadium,
                   m.is_home, m.goals_for, m.goals_against, m.match_status
              FROM dim_match m
              JOIN dim_stadium s ON s.stadium_id=m.stadium_id
             WHERE (p_date_from IS NULL OR m.match_date >= TRUNC(p_date_from))
               AND (p_date_to IS NULL OR m.match_date < TRUNC(p_date_to)+1)
             ORDER BY m.match_date;
    END;

    PROCEDURE list_available_tickets(p_match_id IN NUMBER,
                                     p_result OUT SYS_REFCURSOR) IS
    BEGIN
        OPEN p_result FOR
            SELECT t.ticket_id, t.match_id, s.sector_code, t.seat_row,
                   t.seat_number, t.price, t.status
              FROM tickets t
              JOIN dim_sector s ON s.sector_id=t.sector_id
             WHERE t.match_id=p_match_id
               AND (t.status='FREE' OR
                    (t.status='RESERVED' AND t.reserved_until <= SYSDATE))
             ORDER BY s.sector_code, t.seat_row, t.seat_number;
    END;

    PROCEDURE list_merch(p_result OUT SYS_REFCURSOR) IS
    BEGIN
        OPEN p_result FOR
            SELECT merch_id, name, category, price, description
              FROM dim_merch
             WHERE is_active='Y'
             ORDER BY category, name;
    END;

    PROCEDURE reserve_ticket(p_ticket_id IN NUMBER, p_app_user_id IN NUMBER,
                             p_minutes IN NUMBER DEFAULT 15) IS
        l_fan_id NUMBER;
        l_status VARCHAR2(10);
        l_reserved_until DATE;
    BEGIN
        IF p_minutes IS NULL OR p_minutes < 1 OR p_minutes > 120 THEN
            RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20042,'Срок резерва должен составлять от 1 до 120 минут');
        END IF;
        l_fan_id := current_fan_id(p_app_user_id);
        SELECT status, reserved_until
          INTO l_status, l_reserved_until
          FROM tickets
         WHERE ticket_id=p_ticket_id
           FOR UPDATE;

        IF l_status='RESERVED' AND l_reserved_until <= SYSDATE THEN
            UPDATE tickets
               SET status='FREE', reserved_by=NULL, reserved_until=NULL
             WHERE ticket_id=p_ticket_id;
            l_status := 'FREE';
        END IF;

        IF l_status NOT IN ('FREE','RETURNED') THEN
            RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20043,'Билет уже продан или зарезервирован');
        END IF;

        UPDATE tickets
           SET status='RESERVED', fan_id=NULL, sale_time_id=NULL,
               reserved_by=l_fan_id, reserved_until=SYSDATE+(p_minutes/1440)
         WHERE ticket_id=p_ticket_id;
    EXCEPTION
        WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN
            RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20044,'Билет не найден');
    END;

    PROCEDURE add_favorite(p_app_user_id IN NUMBER, p_player_id IN NUMBER) IS
        l_fan_id NUMBER;
    BEGIN
        l_fan_id := current_fan_id(p_app_user_id);
        INSERT INTO fan_player_favorites(fan_id,player_id)
        VALUES(l_fan_id,p_player_id);
    EXCEPTION
        WHEN DUP_VAL_ON_INDEX THEN
            RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20045,'Игрок уже находится в списке избранного');
    END;

    PROCEDURE remove_favorite(p_app_user_id IN NUMBER, p_player_id IN NUMBER) IS
        l_fan_id NUMBER;
    BEGIN
        l_fan_id := current_fan_id(p_app_user_id);
        DELETE FROM fan_player_favorites
         WHERE fan_id=l_fan_id AND player_id=p_player_id;
        IF SQL%ROWCOUNT=0 THEN
            RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20046,'Игрок не найден в списке избранного');
        END IF;
    END;

    PROCEDURE list_favorites(p_app_user_id IN NUMBER, p_result OUT SYS_REFCURSOR) IS
        l_fan_id NUMBER;
    BEGIN
        l_fan_id := current_fan_id(p_app_user_id);
        OPEN p_result FOR
            SELECT p.player_id,p.first_name,p.last_name,p.position,f.created_at
              FROM fan_player_favorites f
              JOIN dim_player p ON p.player_id=f.player_id
             WHERE f.fan_id=l_fan_id
             ORDER BY f.created_at DESC;
    END;

END pkg_user_api;
/

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Управление прикладными учётными записями. Доступ получает только
-- главная административная роль Oracle.
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE OR REPLACE PACKAGE pkg_security_api AUTHID DEFINER AS
    PROCEDURE create_app_user(
        p_username IN VARCHAR2, p_password_hash IN VARCHAR2,
        p_email IN VARCHAR2, p_role IN VARCHAR2,
        p_fan_id IN NUMBER, p_user_id OUT NUMBER);
    PROCEDURE change_app_user_role(p_user_id IN NUMBER, p_role IN VARCHAR2);
    PROCEDURE set_app_user_active(p_user_id IN NUMBER, p_is_active IN CHAR);
    PROCEDURE bind_app_user_db_user(p_user_id IN NUMBER, p_db_username IN VARCHAR2);
END pkg_security_api;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE PACKAGE BODY pkg_security_api AS
    PROCEDURE create_app_user(
        p_username IN VARCHAR2, p_password_hash IN VARCHAR2,
        p_email IN VARCHAR2, p_role IN VARCHAR2,
        p_fan_id IN NUMBER, p_user_id OUT NUMBER) IS
        l_role VARCHAR2(20);
    BEGIN
        l_role := UPPER(TRIM(p_role));
        IF l_role NOT IN ('MAIN_ADMIN','ADMIN','USER') THEN
            RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20050,'Недопустимая прикладная роль');
        END IF;
        IF p_password_hash IS NULL OR LENGTH(TRIM(p_password_hash)) < 20 THEN
            RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20051,'Передайте хеш пароля, а не открытый пароль');
        END IF;
        INSERT INTO app_users(username,password_hash,email,role,fan_id)
        VALUES(TRIM(p_username),p_password_hash,LOWER(TRIM(p_email)),l_role,p_fan_id)
        RETURNING user_id INTO p_user_id;
    END;

    PROCEDURE change_app_user_role(p_user_id IN NUMBER, p_role IN VARCHAR2) IS
        l_role VARCHAR2(20);
    BEGIN
        l_role := UPPER(TRIM(p_role));
        IF l_role NOT IN ('MAIN_ADMIN','ADMIN','USER') THEN
            RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20050,'Недопустимая прикладная роль');
        END IF;
        UPDATE app_users SET role=l_role WHERE user_id=p_user_id;
        IF SQL%ROWCOUNT=0 THEN
            RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20052,'Прикладная учётная запись не найдена');
        END IF;
    END;

    PROCEDURE set_app_user_active(p_user_id IN NUMBER, p_is_active IN CHAR) IS
    BEGIN
        IF UPPER(p_is_active) NOT IN ('Y','N') THEN
            RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20053,'Признак активности должен быть Y или N');
        END IF;
        UPDATE app_users SET is_active=UPPER(p_is_active) WHERE user_id=p_user_id;
        IF SQL%ROWCOUNT=0 THEN
            RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20052,'Прикладная учётная запись не найдена');
        END IF;
    END;

    PROCEDURE bind_app_user_db_user(p_user_id IN NUMBER, p_db_username IN VARCHAR2) IS
        l_count PLS_INTEGER;
    BEGIN
        IF p_db_username IS NULL OR TRIM(p_db_username) IS NULL THEN
            RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20054,'Необходимо указать Oracle-пользователя');
        END IF;
        SELECT COUNT(*) INTO l_count
          FROM all_users
         WHERE username=UPPER(TRIM(p_db_username));
        IF l_count=0 THEN
            RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20055,'Указанный Oracle-пользователь не существует');
        END IF;
        UPDATE app_users
           SET db_username=UPPER(TRIM(p_db_username))
         WHERE user_id=p_user_id;
        IF SQL%ROWCOUNT=0 THEN
            RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20052,'Прикладная учётная запись не найдена');
        END IF;
    END;
END pkg_security_api;
/

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Импорт/экспорт JSON-файлов. Ожидается файл UTF-8 с объектом вида
-- {"players":[{...}, ...]}. Для файлов используется DIRECTORY HK_JSON_DIR.
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE OR REPLACE PACKAGE pkg_json_io AUTHID DEFINER AS
    PROCEDURE import_players(p_file_name IN VARCHAR2);
    PROCEDURE export_players(p_file_name IN VARCHAR2);
END pkg_json_io;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE PACKAGE BODY pkg_json_io AS
    PROCEDURE read_file_to_clob(p_file_name IN VARCHAR2, p_json OUT CLOB) IS
        l_file UTL_FILE.FILE_TYPE;
        l_line VARCHAR2(32767);
    BEGIN
        DBMS_LOB.CREATETEMPORARY(p_json, TRUE);
        l_file := UTL_FILE.FOPEN('HK_JSON_DIR',p_file_name,'R',32767);
        LOOP
            BEGIN
                UTL_FILE.GET_LINE(l_file,l_line);
                DBMS_LOB.WRITEAPPEND(p_json,LENGTH(l_line),l_line);
                DBMS_LOB.WRITEAPPEND(p_json,1,CHR(10));
            EXCEPTION
                WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN EXIT;
            END;
        END LOOP;
        UTL_FILE.FCLOSE(l_file);
    EXCEPTION
        WHEN OTHERS THEN
            IF UTL_FILE.IS_OPEN(l_file) THEN UTL_FILE.FCLOSE(l_file); END IF;
            IF p_json IS NOT NULL AND DBMS_LOB.ISTEMPORARY(p_json)=1 THEN
                DBMS_LOB.FREETEMPORARY(p_json);
            END IF;
            RAISE;
    END;

    PROCEDURE import_players(p_file_name IN VARCHAR2) IS
        l_json CLOB;
    BEGIN
        read_file_to_clob(p_file_name,l_json);
        INSERT INTO dim_player(first_name,last_name,position,jersey_number,
                               birth_date,country,height_cm,weight_kg)
        SELECT jt.first_name,
               jt.last_name,
               UPPER(jt.position),
               jt.jersey_number,
               CASE WHEN jt.birth_date IS NOT NULL
                    THEN TO_DATE(jt.birth_date,'YYYY-MM-DD') END,
               jt.country,
               jt.height_cm,
               jt.weight_kg
          FROM JSON_TABLE(l_json,'$.players[*]'
                 COLUMNS (
                   first_name    VARCHAR2(50) PATH '$.first_name',
                   last_name     VARCHAR2(50) PATH '$.last_name',
                   position      VARCHAR2(15) PATH '$.position',
                   jersey_number NUMBER       PATH '$.jersey_number' NULL ON ERROR,
                   birth_date    VARCHAR2(10) PATH '$.birth_date' NULL ON ERROR,
                   country       VARCHAR2(50) PATH '$.country' NULL ON ERROR,
                   height_cm     NUMBER       PATH '$.height_cm' NULL ON ERROR,
                   weight_kg     NUMBER       PATH '$.weight_kg' NULL ON ERROR
                 )) jt;
        DBMS_LOB.FREETEMPORARY(l_json);
    EXCEPTION
        WHEN OTHERS THEN
            IF l_json IS NOT NULL AND DBMS_LOB.ISTEMPORARY(l_json)=1 THEN
                DBMS_LOB.FREETEMPORARY(l_json);
            END IF;
            RAISE;
    END;

    PROCEDURE export_players(p_file_name IN VARCHAR2) IS
        l_json CLOB;
        l_file UTL_FILE.FILE_TYPE;
        l_offset PLS_INTEGER := 1;
        l_chunk VARCHAR2(32767);
        l_length PLS_INTEGER;
    BEGIN
        SELECT JSON_OBJECT(
                 'players' VALUE (
                   SELECT JSON_ARRAYAGG(
                            JSON_OBJECT(
                              'player_id' VALUE player_id,
                              'first_name' VALUE first_name,
                              'last_name' VALUE last_name,
                              'position' VALUE position,
                              'jersey_number' VALUE jersey_number,
                              'birth_date' VALUE CASE WHEN birth_date IS NOT NULL
                                                       THEN TO_CHAR(birth_date,'YYYY-MM-DD') END,
                              'country' VALUE country,
                              'height_cm' VALUE height_cm,
                              'weight_kg' VALUE weight_kg,
                              'is_active' VALUE is_active
                              NULL ON NULL RETURNING CLOB
                            ) RETURNING CLOB
                          )
                     FROM dim_player
                 ) FORMAT JSON
                 RETURNING CLOB
               )
          INTO l_json
          FROM dual;

        l_file := UTL_FILE.FOPEN('HK_JSON_DIR',p_file_name,'W',32767);
        l_length := DBMS_LOB.GETLENGTH(l_json);
        WHILE l_offset <= l_length LOOP
            l_chunk := DBMS_LOB.SUBSTR(l_json,32767,l_offset);
            UTL_FILE.PUT(l_file,l_chunk);
            l_offset := l_offset + LENGTH(l_chunk);
        END LOOP;
        UTL_FILE.NEW_LINE(l_file);
        UTL_FILE.FCLOSE(l_file);
        DBMS_LOB.FREETEMPORARY(l_json);
    EXCEPTION
        WHEN OTHERS THEN
            IF UTL_FILE.IS_OPEN(l_file) THEN UTL_FILE.FCLOSE(l_file); END IF;
            IF l_json IS NOT NULL AND DBMS_LOB.ISTEMPORARY(l_json)=1 THEN
                DBMS_LOB.FREETEMPORARY(l_json);
            END IF;
            RAISE;
    END;
END pkg_json_io;
/

PROMPT Пакеты PKG_ADMIN_API, PKG_USER_API, PKG_SECURITY_API и PKG_JSON_IO созданы.

                     month_name,quarter_num,year_num,season)
SELECT TO_NUMBER(TO_CHAR(d,'YYYYMMDD')),
       d,
       TO_CHAR(d,'FMDAY','NLS_DATE_LANGUAGE=RUSSIAN'),
       TO_NUMBER(TO_CHAR(d,'DD')),
       TO_NUMBER(TO_CHAR(d,'MM')),
       TO_CHAR(d,'FMMONTH','NLS_DATE_LANGUAGE=RUSSIAN'),
       TO_NUMBER(TO_CHAR(d,'Q')),
       TO_NUMBER(TO_CHAR(d,'YYYY')),
       CASE WHEN TO_NUMBER(TO_CHAR(d,'MM')) >= 7
            THEN TO_CHAR(TO_NUMBER(TO_CHAR(d,'YYYY'))) || '/' ||
                 TO_CHAR(TO_NUMBER(TO_CHAR(d,'YYYY'))+1)
            ELSE TO_CHAR(TO_NUMBER(TO_CHAR(d,'YYYY'))-1) || '/' ||
                 TO_CHAR(TO_NUMBER(TO_CHAR(d,'YYYY')))
       END
  FROM (SELECT DATE '2024-01-01' + LEVEL - 1 AS d
          FROM dual
        CONNECT BY LEVEL <= (DATE '2035-12-31' - DATE '2024-01-01') + 1);

================================================================================
FILE: HK_DINAMO_Oracle19c\06_performance\07_load_100k_tickets.sql
================================================================================
-- =====================================================================
-- Загрузка минимум 100 000 тестовых строк в TICKETS.
-- Выполнять от HK_DINAMO после seed_demo_data.sql.
-- Десять тестовых матчей, по 10 000 мест на матч (2 сектора по 5 000).
-- =====================================================================
WHENEVER SQLERROR EXIT SQL.SQLCODE
SET SERVEROUTPUT ON

DECLARE
    l_stadium_id NUMBER;
    l_sector_a   NUMBER;
    l_sector_b   NUMBER;
    l_fan_id     NUMBER;
    l_match_id   NUMBER;
    l_time_id    NUMBER(8);
    l_existing   NUMBER;
    l_total      NUMBER;
BEGIN
    SELECT COUNT(*) INTO l_existing
      FROM dim_match
     WHERE opponent_name LIKE 'TEST PERF %';
    IF l_existing > 0 THEN
        RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20100,
          'Тестовые матчи уже существуют. Для повторного запуска очистите тестовые матчи и билеты либо создайте новую схему.');
    END IF;

    SELECT MIN(stadium_id) INTO l_stadium_id FROM dim_stadium;
    SELECT MIN(sector_id), MAX(sector_id)
      INTO l_sector_a, l_sector_b
      FROM dim_sector
     WHERE stadium_id=l_stadium_id;
    SELECT MIN(fan_id) INTO l_fan_id FROM dim_fan;

    IF l_stadium_id IS NULL OR l_sector_a IS NULL OR l_sector_b IS NULL OR l_sector_a=l_sector_b OR l_fan_id IS NULL THEN
        RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20101,'Сначала выполните загрузку демонстрационных данных');
    END IF;

    FOR i IN 1..10 LOOP
        SELECT time_id INTO l_time_id
          FROM dim_time
         WHERE full_date=TRUNC(SYSDATE)+i;

        INSERT INTO dim_match(match_date,time_id,opponent_name,stadium_id,is_home)
        VALUES(TRUNC(SYSDATE)+i,l_time_id,'TEST PERF '||TO_CHAR(i,'FM00'),l_stadium_id,'Y')
        RETURNING match_id INTO l_match_id;

        INSERT INTO tickets(match_id,sector_id,seat_row,seat_number,price,status,
                             fan_id,sale_time_id,reserved_by,reserved_until)
        SELECT l_match_id,
               CASE WHEN q.n <= 5000 THEN l_sector_a ELSE l_sector_b END,
               CEIL(q.seat_pos/100),
               MOD(q.seat_pos-1,100)+1,
               40 + MOD(q.n,5)*5,
               CASE MOD(q.n,4)
                    WHEN 0 THEN 'FREE'
                    WHEN 1 THEN 'RESERVED'
                    WHEN 2 THEN 'SOLD'
                    ELSE 'RETURNED'
               END,
               CASE WHEN MOD(q.n,4)=2 THEN l_fan_id END,
               CASE WHEN MOD(q.n,4)=2 THEN l_time_id END,
               CASE WHEN MOD(q.n,4)=1 THEN l_fan_id END,
               CASE WHEN MOD(q.n,4)=1 THEN SYSDATE+1 END
          FROM (SELECT LEVEL AS n,
                       CASE WHEN LEVEL<=5000 THEN LEVEL ELSE LEVEL-5000 END AS seat_pos
                  FROM dual
                CONNECT BY LEVEL<=10000) q;
    END LOOP;

    SELECT COUNT(*) INTO l_total
      FROM tickets t
      JOIN dim_match m ON m.match_id=t.match_id
     WHERE m.opponent_name LIKE 'TEST PERF %';

    IF l_total < 100000 THEN
        RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20102,'Не удалось загрузить требуемые 100 000 строк');
    END IF;

    DBMS_STATS.GATHER_TABLE_STATS(USER,'TICKETS',cascade=>TRUE);
    COMMIT;
    DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE('Загружено строк в TICKETS для тестирования: '||l_total);
END;
/


================================================================================
FILE: HK_DINAMO_Oracle19c\06_performance\08_perf_before_indexes.sql

-- =====================================================================
-- Фиксация плана и времени выполнения ДО создания дополнительного индекса.
-- Выполнять от HK_DINAMO после загрузки 100 000 строк.
-- =====================================================================
SET TIMING ON
SET LINESIZE 180
SET PAGESIZE 100

EXPLAIN PLAN SET STATEMENT_ID='TICKETS_BEFORE_IDX' FOR
SELECT /*+ FULL(t) */ COUNT(*) AS sold_tickets,
       NVL(SUM(t.price),0) AS revenue
  FROM tickets t
 WHERE t.status='SOLD'
   AND t.sale_time_id=(
       SELECT MAX(m.time_id)
         FROM dim_match m
        WHERE m.opponent_name LIKE 'TEST PERF %'
   );

SELECT *
  FROM TABLE(DBMS_XPLAN.DISPLAY(NULL,'TICKETS_BEFORE_IDX','BASIC +COST +ROWS +PREDICATE'));

PROMPT Фактический запуск запроса ДО индексации:

       NVL(SUM(t.price),0) AS revenue
  FROM tickets t
 WHERE t.status='SOLD'
   AND t.sale_time_id=(
       SELECT MAX(m.time_id)
         FROM dim_match m
        WHERE m.opponent_name LIKE 'TEST PERF %'
   );

PROMPT Сохраните время выполнения и план в таблицу результатов курсовой.
SET TIMING OFF

PRINT new_player_id;
COMMIT;

FROM dual;

PRINT rc;

EXEC HK_DINAMO.PKG_ADMIN_API.PLAYER_POPULARITY_REPORT(:rc);
PRINT rc;

================================================================================
FILE: ИНСТРУКЦИЯ_к_исправленному_установщику.txt
================================================================================
Исправленный установщик HK DINAMO
=================================

В архиве файл 01_INSTALL_ALL.ps1 с исправлением ORA-01403.

Как установить исправление:
1. Распакуйте архив.
2. Скопируйте 01_INSTALL_ALL.ps1 в ту же папку, где лежит ваш 01_INSTALL_ALL.bat.
3. Согласитесь на замену старого 01_INSTALL_ALL.ps1.
4. Запустите 01_INSTALL_ALL.bat.

Исправление:
- Скрипт получает фактический список пользовательских PDB из Oracle.
- Если PDB одна, она выбирается автоматически, даже если имя отличается от ORCLPDB1.
- Если PDB несколько, выводятся доступные имена для выбора.
- SQL-блок больше не падает немедленно с ORA-01403 из-за неверного имени PDB.

Важно о диске D:
- Режим 1 создаёт новый контейнер и монтирует D:\HK_DINAMO\Oracle\data в /opt/oracle/oradata.
- Режимы 2 и 3 используют существующий контейнер и не переносят его файлы данных.
  Установщик дополнительно показывает фактическое хранилище существующего контейнера.

Рядом с установщиком должны оставаться папки:
- HK_DINAMO_Oracle19c
- HK_DINAMO_Node_App

СОДЕРЖАНИЕ ЕДИНОГО КОМПЛЕКТА «ДИНАМО-МИНСК»

Frontend веб-приложения: обычные HTML, CSS и JavaScript.
В каталоге HK_DINAMO_Node_App/public находятся index.html, styles.css и app.js.
EJS-шаблонов, каталога views/ и зависимости ejs в комплекте нет.

Node.js + Express предоставляет REST API и подключение к Oracle Database 19c.

Файлы комплекта:
